# Missing inputs

What the library should hold but does not yet, why, where to get it and what to do next. Generated from the `gaps` field
of each card in catalog/technologies/ and from register entries without a card. Agents: call `list_gaps`.

699 missing inputs on 333 cards; 7 register technologies have no card yet.

| not_in_sources_read | 683 |
| paid_source | 11 |
| no_card_yet | 7 |
| status_unchecked | 2 |
| source_unreachable | 2 |
| conflicting_sources | 1 |

| [Allam-Fetvedt cycle (oxy-combustion supercritical CO2 power)](technologies-CCS.md#ccs-allam-cycle) | whether any utility-scale Allam-Fetvedt cycle plant is still planned (status) | NET Power's March 2026 update describes its first commercial project as gas turbines with post-combustion capture; it does not say when or whether an Allam-cycle plant follows. RESOLVED NEGATIVE 2026-10-28: no utility-scale Allam-cycle plant is under construction or firmly planned. NET Power is running demonstration-plant testing campaigns through 2026 (FY2025 10-K, Mar 2024), and in January 2026 withdrew the interconnection request for its proposed Midcontinent clean power hub (Energy Intelligence); the MIT CCS project database lists the 295 MW scale-up as cancelled/inactive. No open source states a capital cost per kW for a modular/containerised engine plant, or a premium/discount against a stick-built plant: EIA's AEO2025 capital-cost report has no reciprocating-engine or modular line to cite, the Danish Energy Agency catalogue page 404s, ESMAP's equipment-price study is 403, and vendor pages claim only qualitative savings (Wärtsilä's '20-30% lower CAPEX' names no comparator). The EPA and DOE engine figures now on pwr-gas-engines are engine CHP, not a modular premium. | Manufacturer investor material or EPC tender results comparing modular and stick-built engine plants (open)<br>Owner/utility board papers publishing both bids for the same project (open) | Treat as unmeasured. Only an owner/utility board paper publishing both bids for one project, or a manufacturer's investor disclosure, can close this; do not derive it by subtracting unrelated figures. |
| [Modular reciprocating engine power plants](technologies-PWR.md#pwr-modular-engine-plants) | TRL with a cited source (trl) | No agency assigns a TRL or maturity grade to modular engine plants as distinct from the engine itself: the IEA Clean Energy Technology Guide dataset has no such row, and maturity schemes (IEA, DOE, EPA) grade reciprocating/gas engines generically. Commercial deployment is evidenced by the reference plants (Dohazari 102 MW in 10 months; KPUB 124 MW), so the blocker is the missing agency rating, not the technology. | IEA ETP Clean Energy Technology Guide (collected): search for reciprocating engine power plants (catalog: iea-reports, open) | Leave the TRL empty rather than infer 9; cite the deployment record in prose if a number is demanded. |
